# Down on the Street

Journal: `quests/main_quest/act_01/q112_01_old_friend` · `MainQuest` · hash `3514555831`.

Journal premise: If someone told me I'd be taking calls from Saburo Arasaka's fucking bodyguard, I'd've laughed in their face. And now we're supposed to meet with some Takemura? Hand over all our detes? I'm not even gonna pretend like this is a good idea. But fuck it – sometimes you just gotta go all in.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 65 exact journal-linked nodes in 14 resources; 18 resources in the inspected dependency context.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.
